*Taehyung Yu,Seongjae Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现零样本语音合成中Best-of-N推理的ASR验证器存在同族偏差问题（同一模型家族的验证器与评估器配对会高估性能），并提出跨族排名集成方法（排名平均与合取最大排名），在三个独立评估器上取得最低平均WER 1.61%，推荐将跨评估器三角验证作为默认报告实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Best-of-N推理依赖ASR验证器筛选候选，但验证器的表观质量强烈依赖于使用哪个ASR家族作为评估器，存在未被充分探索的评估混淆因素。

Method: 提出两种跨族排名集成方法：排名平均（rank-averaging）和合取最大排名（conjunctive max-rank），在不同ASR家族（Whisper、wav2vec 2.0、HuBERT）间融合验证器排名。

Result: 跨族排名集成在N=10时达到最低平均WER 1.61%（较F5-TTS基线降低12%），最佳单验证器在官方F5-TTS评估器下将WER从2.06%降至1.72%（-16.5%）。

Conclusion: 跨评估器三角验证应作为默认报告实践，以消除同族验证器-评估器配对带来的性能高估偏差。

Abstract: Best-of-$N$ (BoN) inference improves content consistency in zero-shot text-to-speech by selecting from $N$ candidates with an automatic speech recognition (ASR) verifier. We identify an underexplored evaluation confound: a verifier's apparent quality depends strongly on which ASR family judges it. On LibriSpeech-PC test-clean~\citep{librispeechpc} with F5-TTS~\citep{f5tts}, verifier rankings reverse across Whisper, wav2vec~2.0, and HuBERT evaluators, and same-family verifier-evaluator pairs recover 2-3$\times$ more oracle headroom than cross-family pairs despite near-identical representations (linear CKA $0.978$) -- a pattern consistent with identity- or lineage-level coupling rather than representational overlap. We propose two \textbf{cross-family rank ensembles} (rank-averaging and conjunctive max-rank) that attain the lowest mean WER across three independent evaluators -- $1.61\%$ at $N{=}10$ ($-12\%$ relative to F5-TTS) -- with no measurable degradation under automatic SIM-o/UTMOS metrics; the best single verifier drives WER from $2.06\%$ to $1.72\%$ ($-16.5\%$) under the official F5-TTS evaluator. We recommend cross-evaluator triangulation as default reporting practice.

</details>


### [27] [XALPHA: A Memory-Driven AI Quant Researcher for Hypothesis-to-Code Alpha Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08332)
*Fengyuan Liu,Yuchen Fu,Yuqi Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: XAlpha 是一个基于记忆驱动的 AI 量化研究员系统，通过多源研究记忆系统（整合报告知识、发现反馈）和多脑架构（宏观脑规划主题、微观脑实现验证、交叉脑总结反馈），将阿尔法挖掘从孤立的因子生成转变为持续闭环研究过程，在 CSI300 上表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的阿尔法发现方法大多自动化孤立步骤，而非作为端到端的量化研究员运行；它们无法吸收外部知识、闭合从假设到代码的验证循环，也无法从累积的发现反馈中学习。

Method: XAlpha 采用多源研究记忆系统（整合报告基础金融知识与先前发现反馈），通过宏观脑（规划研究主题、选择原型）、微观脑（将假设池转化为可执行因子代码并验证假设-代码-金融合理性三者对齐）、交叉脑（将实证结果整合为生成级反馈、周期级摘要和原型级研究线索）的三脑协作架构，实现闭环的阿尔法发现流程。

Result: 在 CSI300 上的实验表明，XAlpha 在整体阿尔法发现性能上优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: XAlpha 将阿尔法挖掘从孤立的因子生成转变为持续闭环研究过程（读取、假设、实现、验证、反思、进化），证明了基于记忆驱动的端到端 AI 量化研究员在金融信号发现中的有效性。

Abstract: Financial markets are noisy, non-stationary, and high-dimensional, making it difficult to discover predictive and robust trading signals. Alpha discovery has evolved from manual factor design to machine learning, evolutionary search, and recent LLM-based frameworks, improving the efficiency of factor generation, search, and evaluation. However, existing methods still mostly automate isolated steps, rather than functioning as end-to-end quant researchers that can absorb external knowledge, close the hypothesis-to-code validation loop, and learn from accumulated discovery feedback. To fill this gap, we introduce XAlpha, a memory-driven AI Quant Researcher for continuous hypothesis-to-code alpha discovery. XAlpha maintains a multi-source research memory system that integrates report-grounded financial knowledge with discovery feedback from prior generations and research cycles. Guided by this memory system, a Macro Brain plans research themes and selects suitable Archetypes; a Micro Brain transforms the planned hypothesis pool into executable factor code and verifies ex-ante tri-alignment among the hypothesis idea, code logic, and financial plausibility; and a Cross Brain consolidates empirical outcomes into generation-level feedback, cycle-level summaries, and archetype-level research cues for future exploration. In this way, XAlpha turns alpha mining from isolated factor generation into a closed-loop research process that continuously reads, hypothesizes, implements, validates, reflects, and evolves. Experiments on CSI300 show that XAlpha achieves stronger overall alpha discovery performance than representative baselines.

</details>


### [28] [TypeProbe: Recovering Type Representations from Hidden States of Pre-trained Code Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08339)
*Giuliano Gorgone,Fausto Carcassi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过探针方法研究预训练代码模型在残差流中是否编码跨语言的类型信息，发现即使在不含类型标注的代码中，模型也能学习到跨语言的类型表征，并且这种结构对词汇扰动和跨语言句法变化具有一定的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型虽性能优异，但其内部如何编码类型信息尚不清楚，且此前可解释性工作未直接针对形式类型语义或跨语言类型表征展开研究。

Method: 使用Java和Python的平行代码数据集，通过探针分析预训练代码模型残差流中的内部类型表征；训练一个语言上的探针来推断另一语言的参数和结果类型，检验隐状态是否线性编码函数应用隐含的结果类型。

Result: 跨语言类型表征即使在无类型代码中也能涌现；隐状态线性编码了函数应用的结果类型；该结构对词汇扰动和跨语言句法变化具有部分鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 预训练代码模型内部存在跨语言的类型表示结构，这为理解代码模型的泛化能力和可解释性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: State-of-the-art code models achieve impressive performance, yet the extent to which they internally encode type information remains poorly understood. We probe the residual streams of pretrained code models for internal type representations using a parallel dataset of Java and Python code examples. Our results show that cross-lingual type representations emerge even from untyped code. Moreover, we test whether hidden states linearly encode the result type implied by typed function application by training probes on one language to infer argument and result types in the other. Finally, we find that this structure is partly robust to lexical perturbations and cross-language syntactic variations. To the best of our knowledge, prior work on interpretability of code models has not directly targeted formal type semantics or cross-lingual type representations. We release our code and datasets.

</details>


### [29] [Grounded Event Extraction from SEC 8-K Filings with a Fine-Grained Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08346)
*Rian Dolphin,Joe Dursun,Jarrett Blankenship,Katie Adams,Quinton Pike*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段系统，利用大语言模型对SEC 8-K文件进行细粒度事件标签化（119种事件类型），通过引用锚定和质量评分实现高精度，并在292,984份文件中生成601,088个可追溯标签，事件研究验证了该分类法的经济区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEC 8-K文件是上市公司重大事件的主要披露渠道，但其SEC项目代码粒度粗糙，单一代码涵盖从例行行政变更到CEO离职等广泛事件，且许多市场敏感披露落入模糊的兜底项。现有方法缺乏对大规模语料进行细粒度、可追溯标签的有效手段。

Method: 两阶段系统：第一阶段通过模糊n-gram验证将输出约束到有效分类条目，并将每个标签锚定到逐字引文；第二阶段对每条引文按类别定义重新评级，产生质量分数。应用于2022-2026年292,984份文件。通过LLM法官评估5,125个分层标签的精确率，并通过事件研究验证分类法的经济区分能力。

Result: 生成601,088个可追溯事件标签。质量分数从12%到96%单调提升精确率，无支持标签从8%降至接近零。消融实验表明质量分数仅在专用第二遍中分配时才校准。事件研究证实分类法能够分离共享同一项目代码但经济意义不同的事件。

Conclusion: 该系统实现了大规模、高精度、可追溯的8-K文件细粒度标签化，质量分数提供了可靠的置信度指标，分类法具有实际的经济区分价值。

Abstract: Form 8-K filings are the primary channel through which U.S. public companies disclose material events, but the SEC item codes attached to them are coarse: a single item spans routine administrative changes and chief executive departures, and many of the most market-moving disclosures fall into a catch-all item. Large language models make fine-grained labelling feasible at corpus scale, but only if the labels can be traced to the source text and shown to be reliable. We present a two-stage system that tags 8-K disclosures against a three-tier taxonomy of 119 event types. The first stage constrains output to valid taxonomy entries and anchors every tag to a verbatim quote via fuzzy n-gram validation; the second re-grades each cited quote against the category definition to produce a quality score. Applying the system to 292,984 filings from 2022 to 2026 yields 601,088 grounded event tags, which we release. Over 5,125 stratified tags, an LLM judge finds precision rises monotonically with the quality score, from 12% to 96%, while unsupported tags fall from 8% to near zero. Ablation shows the score is calibrated only when assigned in a dedicated second pass. An event study on unsigned abnormal returns confirms, without any language model, that the taxonomy separates economically distinct events sharing an item code.

</details>


### [30] [Echoes Across Vietnam's Highlands, Delta, and Coast: A Multilingual Corpus for Cham, Khmer, and Tay-Nung](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08362)
*Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Vinh Cong Doan,Tai Tien Ta,Khoa Duc Anh Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对越南少数民族语言（Cham、Khmer、Tay-Nung）在NLP领域几乎空白的问题，构建了首个语料库CKTN（44,367文档，24M子词token），发现多语言编码器严重碎片化且常用评估指标具有误导性，提出脚本感知的适应方案——词汇增强+校准替换token预训练，有效降低碎片化并提升分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越南少数民族语言在NLP领域几乎空白，除数据稀缺外，这些语言在文字系统、越南语接触程度和标准化程度上存在显著差异，标准多语言适应方法可能学习到错误信号，亟需针对性的语料库和适应方法。

Method: 构建CKTN语料库（44,367文档，24M子词token），覆盖持续预训练、类别分类和摘要-文档检索三个任务；提出脚本感知的适应方案：词汇增强（vocabulary augmentation）+校准替换token预训练（calibrated replaced-token pretraining），防止判别器利用简单的脚本不匹配进行欺骗。

Result: 现有多语言编码器对这些语言存在严重碎片化；常见适应指标具有误导性（如降低语言建模损失或在词汇重叠检索上表现好，但文档级语义泛化却失败）；提出的脚本感知方法显著降低碎片化，在分类性能上超越所有对比模型，同时揭示了词汇重叠检索作为评估信号的局限性。

Conclusion: 脚本感知的适应方法（词汇增强+校准替换token预训练）能有效解决多语言编码器对越南少数民族语言的碎片化问题，提升语义泛化能力；词汇重叠检索不适合作为语义理解的评估信号。

Abstract: Vietnam's ethnic minority languages are almost absent from the field of Natural Language Processing (NLP), and the challenge goes beyond data scarcity: Cham, Khmer, and Tay-Nung differ sharply in script, Vietnamese contact, and standardization, conditions under which standard multilingual adaptation can learn the wrong signals. We introduce CKTN, the first corpus and benchmark for these languages (44,367 documents, 24M subword tokens), spanning continued pretraining, category classification, and summary-document retrieval. We show that existing multilingual encoders severely fragment these languages, and that common adaptation metrics can mislead: models may lower language-modeling loss or excel at lexical-overlap retrieval while still failing at semantic generalization across documents. We address this with a script-aware adaptation recipe - vocabulary augmentation combined with calibrated replaced-token pretraining - that prevents the discriminator from exploiting trivial script mismatches. The result is an encoder with substantially less fragmentation and the strongest classification performance among evaluated models, exposing the limits of lexical-overlap retrieval as an evaluation signal.

</details>


### [31] [Large-Language-Models-as-a-Judge in Theory-Agnostic Adaptive Metric-Alignment for Prototypical Networks in Personality Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08374)
*Jing Jie Tan,Ban-Hoe Kwan,Danny Wee-Kiat Ng,Yan-Chai Hum,Shih-Yu Lo,Po-An Chen,Noriyuki Kawarazaki,Kosuke Takano,Anissa Mokraoui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出JAM框架，一种理论无关的人格识别方法，通过自适应度量对齐和图原型网络，从文本中学习统一的潜在心理表征，无需依赖预定义人格理论标签，并在跨框架泛化上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人格识别方法受限于理论依赖，模型只能适配预定义的人格分类体系（如大五人格等），但人格本质上是理论无关的（theory-invariant），现有标注仅反映潜在特质的片面且不一致的视图，这限制了模型的泛化能力。因此，需要一种理论无关的框架来发现共享的潜在心理结构。

Method: JAM框架包含三个核心组件：(1) Attention-Pooled Graph Prototypical Network，通过嵌入空间中的聚类学习结构化表征；(2) Cross-Theory Harmonization (CTH)，包含Human-Guided Linkage和Machine-Induced Consensus两个子模块，用于统一异构数据集而不依赖预定义标签；(3) LLM-as-a-Judge机制，包含LLM-before-the-loop和LLM-in-the-loop两种配置，识别模糊样本以引导自适应度量学习。

Result: 实验表明JAM在跨框架泛化和性能上均优于基线方法，为理论无关的人格推断建立了强有力的基础，并支持低资源人格理论场景。

Conclusion: JAM成功将人格识别的学习目标从适配预定义理论转向发现统一的潜在心理伪面（pseudo-facets），实现了理论无关的人格推断，证明了通过跨理论协调和自适应度量学习可以有效捕捉共享的心理结构，为未来人格计算研究提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Personality recognition has traditionally been constrained by theory-dependent formulations, where models are trained to fit predefined psychological taxonomies rather than uncovering shared underlying behavioral structure. This limits generalization, as personality itself is better understood as theory-invariant, while existing annotations reflect only partial and sometimes inconsistent views of the same latent traits. In this work, we introduce JAM ((J)udge for (A)daptive (M)etric-Alignment), a theory-agnostic framework that shifts learning from adapting to predefined personality theories toward discovering unified latent pseudo-facets that capture shared psychological structure. Rather than constraining the model to any personality taxonomy during training or inference, the framework learns generalizable psychological representations and can infer an individual's latent psychological profile directly from the textual samples, without requiring theory-specific labels. JAM achieves this through an Attention-Pooled Graph Prototypical Network that learns structured representations via clustering in embedding space, together with a Cross-Theory Harmonization (CTH) approach that integrates (i) Human-Guided Linkage and (ii) Machine-Induced Consensus to unify heterogeneous datasets without relying on predefined labels. To further improve robustness and data quality, we incorporate an LLM-as-a-Judge mechanism operating in two configurations, (i) LLM-before-the-loop and (ii) LLM-in-the-loop which identifies ambiguous samples to guide adaptive metric learning. Experiments show that JAM improves cross-framework generalization and performance, establishing a strong step toward theory-agnostic personality inference and supporting low-resource personality theories. The related code repository, model weights, and artifacts are available at https://research.jingjietan.com/JAM

</details>


### [32] [Prompt Compression via Activation Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08399)
*Thibaud Ardoin,Semira Einsele,Evis Bregu,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将指令提示中的任务相关信息压缩为单个激活向量，并将其注入LLM早期层以替代原始token序列，在精度损失低于2%的情况下实现计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否将指令提示中的任务相关信息压缩为单个激活向量并重新注入模型，从而无需每次处理完整的token序列，减少固定指令提示的每查询计算量。

Method: 使用可学习的加权和从中间层提取激活，并将其注入目标LLM的早期层，实现信息压缩与再注入。

Result: 压缩向量保留了任务相关信息，相对于完整提示处理，精度下降低于2%。实验揭示了LLM激活空间的三个结构特性：中间层表示可有意义地转移到早期层（跨层兼容性）；单个激活向量编码可量化的语义信息；激活的加权和是鲁棒的表示压缩器。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅具有实际意义（减少固定指令提示的每查询计算量），还揭示了LLM激活空间的跨层信息兼容性、语义编码可量化性以及加权和作为压缩器的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models process prompts by propagating activations through dozens of layers before generating a response. We ask whether the task-relevant information contained in an instruction prompt can be compressed into a single activation vector and re-injected into the model, replacing the original token sequence? We show this is achievable using a learned weighted sum of activations extracted at an intermediate layer and injected at an early layer of the target LLM. The compressed vector preserves task-relevant information, incurring an accuracy drop of under $2\%$ relative to full prompt processing. Beyond its practical implications, including reducing per-query computation for fixed instruction prompts without reprocessing the original token sequence, our analysis reveals structure in the activation space of LLMs: (i) mid-layer representations transfer meaningfully to early layers, suggesting a degree of cross-layer compatibility in how information is encoded; (ii) a single activation vector encodes a quantifiable and recoverable amount of semantic information; (iii) a weighted sum of activations is a robust representation compressor.

</details>


### [33] [When Synthetic Speech Is All You Have: Better Call GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08409)
*Shashi Kumar,Yanis Labrak,Hasindri Watawana,Sergio Burdisso,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Kadri Hacioğlu,Petr Motlicek,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用GRPO强化学习方法替代传统SFT进行领域自适应ASR，在仅使用合成语音数据的情况下，将WER相对降低40%-45%，并揭示了GRPO通过改善停止校准和语音-文本对齐来减少插入错误的行为级改进机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行等受监管领域的ASR受限于隐私法规，真实语音数据采集成本高且受法律约束，合成TTS数据成为替代方案但存在声学失配问题，现有研究仅停留在SFT框架下，未能充分挖掘合成数据的潜力。

Method: 采用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）强化学习方法，这是一种无评论家（critic-free）的优化方法，以低WER假设作为奖励信号；对比SFT、GRPO以及SFT+GRPO组合策略，在仅使用合成语音数据条件下进行领域自适应。

Result: GRPO在合成数据上相比SFT实现WER从36.71%降至22.09%（相对降低40%）；SFT+GRPO组合进一步将WER降低45%；性能提升源于行为层面（改进停止校准减少插入错误、优化注意力锚定增强语音-文本对齐），而非早期层表示变化。

Conclusion: 当合成语音是主要可用资源时，强化学习（特别是GRPO）应优先于监督微调用于ASR领域自适应。

Abstract: LLM-based ASR adapted to regulated domains such as banking is bottlenecked by privacy: real speech is costly and legally constrained to collect, making synthetic text-to-speech (TTS) an attractive substitute. Yet synthetic speech stays acoustically mismatched with real recordings, and work on this gap has stayed within supervised fine-tuning (SFT). We instead turn to reinforcement learning, and show that Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) extracts far more from the same synthetic speech than SFT. Synthetic-only adaptation of the model with GRPO, a critic-free method rewarding low-WER hypotheses, reduces WER by 40\% relative to SFT (36.71\%$\to$22.09\%), and an SFT-then-GRPO combination pushes this further to 45\%. We trace the gain to behavior rather than representation: GRPO reduces insertion errors by improving stopping calibration and speech-to-text alignment by better anchoring attention to audio, leaving early-layer representations intact. When synthetic speech is the main resource, reinforcement learning should be preferred over supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [34] [Detecting Ladder Logic Bombs in IEC 61131-3 PLC Programs using ESBMC-PLC+: A Formal Verification Approach with Trigger Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08417)
*Pierre Dantas,Lucas Cordeiro,Waldir Junior*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ESBMC-LLB，一种基于形式化验证的梯形图逻辑炸弹检测方法。它通过建模层暴露功能块逻辑，将炸弹检测转化为安全违规验证问题，支持k-induction无界证明和bounded model checker反例生成。在Iacobelli 2024数据集上检测全部30个炸弹，在SWaT语料库上实现99%检测率（零误报），并明确了语义模型检查与CFG分类技术的互补边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯形图验证器在构建中间表示时会丢弃功能块体内部的逻辑，导致隐藏在功能块中的恶意逻辑（即梯形图逻辑炸弹）无法被检测。签名检测、异常检测和CFG分类技术无法提供无界证明、适应性触发器鲁棒性及精确触发器反例。

Method: 基于ESBMC-PLC+验证引擎，增加建模层暴露功能块逻辑，将炸弹检测形式化为：扫描看门狗暴露非终止负载，输出接线暴露执行器伪造负载为安全违规。使用k-induction进行所有扫描轮次的无界证明，bounded model checker返回触发条件作为反例。支持模拟扩展以处理模拟量逻辑。

Result: 在Iacobelli 2024公共数据集上检测全部30个炸弹并恢复每个触发条件；在PLC-Defuser的SWaT语料库v1.0.0上检测149/150炸弹（99%），零误报；在含非线性非终止炸弹的更新版本中检测率降至49%（SMT求解器超时）。

Conclusion: 语义模型检查与CFG分类是互补技术：前者提供无界证明、适应性触发器鲁棒性，处理布尔/整数和线性模拟逻辑；后者在非线性模拟非终止场景中表现更优。

Abstract: A Ladder Logic Bomb (LLB) is malicious control logic in a Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) program that lies dormant until a trigger activates a payload to manipulate actuators, forge sensor readings, or deny operator control. We observe that real malicious logic hides inside function-block bodies, which existing ladder-diagram verifiers drop from their intermediate representation (IR), making bombs invisible to provers. We present ESBMC-LLB, which uses ESBMC-PLC+ as its verification engine and adds a modeling layer that exposes function-block logic and recasts bomb detection as a formal verification problem: a scan-watchdog exposes non-termination payloads, and output wiring exposes actuator-forgery payloads as safety violations. k-induction gives an unbounded proof of bomb-absence across all scans, and the bounded model checker returns a counterexample that is the trigger - guarantees that signature, anomaly, and CFG-triage detectors lack. On the public Iacobelli 2024 dataset, ESBMC-LLB detects all 30 bombs and recovers every trigger; it also detects adaptive triggers (computed, opaque-arithmetic, multi-scan) that evade CFG-triage. We also report the first semantic model-checker evaluation on PLC-Defuser's SWaT corpus: our analog extension makes the full corpus parseable; on v1.0.0, it detects 149/150 bombs (99%) with zero false positives, recovering each trigger; on a later version with nonlinear non-termination bombs, detection drops to 49% as the SMT solver times out. We conclude that semantic model checking and CFG-triage are complementary - the former gives unbounded proofs, adaptive-trigger robustness, and handles Boolean/integer and linear analog logic; the latter leads to nonlinear analog non-termination, and we delineate where each wins.

</details>


### [35] [Two Axes of LLM Abstention: Answer Correctness and Question Answerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08456)
*Benedikt J. Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，模型的回答置信度与问题可回答性是两条独立的轴。标准置信度阈值无法区分“答错”和“不该答”的情况，而隐藏状态线性探针能检测可回答性但忽视正确性。通过路由指令与探针，可将二者结合为校准策略，显著提升覆盖率与保证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型拒绝机制仅依赖单一置信度阈值，无法区分“答案错误”与“问题本身不可回答（如虚假前提）”这两种应当拒绝的情况，导致模型在虚假前提问题上表现很差，且该盲点不随模型规模扩大而改善。

Method: 在5个指令微调模型（2B-14B）上，分别测量答案置信度（answer-confidence）与隐藏状态线性探针（hidden-state probe）对答案正确性和问题可回答性的检测能力；设计路由策略，将指令与探针结合，形成基于可回答性分数与正确性分数的双阈值校准策略。

Result: 答案置信度对答案正确性敏感但对问题可回答性几乎不敏感，隐藏状态探针则相反；在CREPE虚假前提问题上，置信度指标均接近随机，而探针AUROC达0.69-0.77。直接指令检查前提会导致57%的虚假挑战（误判正确前提），路由探针后挑战精度提升约三倍。双阈值策略在0.75覆盖正确答案时保证预算，单阈值仅0.31；14B规模下单阈值完全失效。

Conclusion: 模型内部表征已经编码了问题可回答性信息，但标准置信度输出无法反映这一点。通过可回答性探针与正确性阈值的分离校准，可以实现更可靠的模型拒绝策略，有效控制不可回答问题回答率，同时保证正确回答覆盖率。

Abstract: A model should refuse two different things: answers it would get wrong, and questions it should not answer at all, such as unanswerable ones or ones resting on a false premise. The usual recipe thresholds a single confidence score, which cannot tell these apart. Across five instruction-tuned models from three families (2B to 14B), we find they are separate axes. Ordinary answer-confidence tracks whether an answer is right but is nearly blind to whether the question is answerable; a linear probe on hidden states does the reverse. The blind spot does not shrink with scale. It is worst on naturally occurring false-premise questions (CREPE). There, answer-confidence, P(IK), P(True), and even asking the model outright whether a premise is false all stay near chance, while a hidden-state probe reaches 0.69 to 0.77 AUROC: the model represents a problem it will not report. This turns out to be fixable. Instructing a model to check premises backfires, because it then disputes sound and false premises alike (57% false challenges), unable to tell them apart; routing the same instruction with the probe roughly triples challenge precision. We turn the two axes into a calibrated policy that answers only when an answerability score and a correctness score each clear a separately certifies behave differently: the unanswerable-answer rate is controllable at every scale, while the wrong-answer rate is capped by model accuracy, so the guarantee tightens as threshold policy certifies both budgets at 0.75 coverage of correct answers, against 0.31 for a single threshold; at 14B it is the only policy that certifies at all.

</details>


### [36] [Cross-seed explainability using Procrustes-conditioned Joint End-to-end Top-K Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08499)
*Bendegúz Váradi,Zoltán Kmetty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种Procrustes条件化的联合端到端Top-K稀疏自编码器，用于从独立训练的BERT模型中提取跨种子的通用特征，在三个基准数据集上实现了比后处理对齐基线更优的通用性（Pearson r ≥ 0.70）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨种子特征通用性是机械可解释性中的基本挑战：由于字典学习是非凸的，独立训练的网络会学到未对齐的特征空间，看似相同的特征可能因随机初始化而不同。

Method: 计算种子间激活空间的Procrustes正交旋转，然后进行联合SAE训练，结合Top-K稀疏性、端到端下游优化以及辅助的死特征复活损失。

Result: 在五个独立种子对（十个BERT模型）和三个基准数据集（SST-2、Stanford Politeness、TweetEval Emotion）上，完整流水线在所有三个数据集上产生的通用特征（Pearson r ≥ 0.70）优于后处理对齐基线。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效提取跨种子的通用特征，且高质量特征编码了可解释的社会语言学模式。

Abstract: We present a Procrustes-conditioned Joint End-to-end Top-K Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) for extracting cross-seed universal features from independently trained BERT models. Cross-seed feature universality is a fundamental challenge in mechanistic interpretability: because dictionary learning is non-convex, independently trained networks learn misaligned feature spaces, so apparently identical features may differ by random initialization. We address this by computing an orthogonal Procrustes rotation between seeds' activation spaces before joint SAE training, combining Top-K sparsity, end-to-end downstream optimization, and an auxiliary dead-feature revival loss based on previous SAE literature. Evaluating on five independent seed pairs (ten BERT models) across three benchmark datasets (SST-2, Stanford Politeness, TweetEval Emotion), our full pipeline produces more universal features (Pearson r $\geq$ 0.70 across seeds) than post-hoc alignment baselines on all three datasets. A minimal qualitative analysis confirms that high-universality features encode interpretable sociolinguistic patterns.

</details>


### [37] [When the Judge Changes, So Does the Measurement: Auditing LLM-as-Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08535)
*Zongyou Yang,Yinghan Hou,Xiaokun Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 评估者替换会导致LLM-as-judge评分变化，即使候选回答固定。研究发现不同升级路径不可互换，仅Qwen3从小到大的相邻升级带来稳健提升，而MiniMax相邻版本间不一致。更强裁判可减少但无法消除位置和冗长偏见，重复陪审团在错误相关时效果有限，结构化辩论缺乏解析器和回退日志难以归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM作为评估者时，评估者（裁判模型）的替换会引入评分歧义，即使被评估的回答不变。作者将此概念化为测量效度问题，旨在系统比较实践中可用的两种升级路径，并量化各类偏差与协议缺陷，进而提出更规范的评估报告标准。

Method: 在四个评判数据集上，对比两种升级路径：Qwen3密集裁判从1.7B到32B参数的规模扩展，以及MiniMax M2-M2.7跨发布版本更新。分析相邻升级的稳健性、位置/冗长偏差的消减效果、重复采样陪审团的误差相关性影响，以及结构化辩论协议的可审计性问题。

Result: 仅Qwen3 1.7B到4B的相邻升级带来稳健增益；MiniMax相邻发布版本间表现不一致。更强裁判减少但未消除位置和冗长偏差。重复采样陪审团在误差相关时收益甚微。结构化辩论能显著改变决策，但缺乏解析器和回退日志时无法将变化归因于真正的审议过程。

Conclusion: 论文主张LLM-as-judge报告应包含：数据集切片、偏差探测、误差依赖性估计以及协议审计轨迹（审计线索）。这些组件是提升评估结果可解释性和可复现性的必要条件。

Abstract: An LLM-as-judge score can move even when the candidate responses stay fixed, simply because the evaluator has changed. We treat this evaluator-replacement ambiguity as a measurement-validity problem. Across four judgment datasets, we compare two upgrade paths available in practice: scaling Qwen3 dense judges from 1.7B to 32B parameters and moving across MiniMax M2-M2.7 released APIs. The main pattern is that judge upgrades are not interchangeable: only Qwen3 1.7B to 4B gives a robust adjacent gain, while MiniMax adjacent releases do not. Stronger judges reduce but do not remove position and verbosity bias. Repeated-sample juries add little when errors are correlated. Structured debate can move decisions substantially, but without parser and fallback logs those shifts cannot be attributed to deliberation. We argue that LLM-as-judge reports should include dataset slices, bias probes, error-dependence estimates, and protocol audit trails.

</details>


### [38] [It Takes a MAESTRO To Prune Bad Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08601)
*Palaash Goel,Ayush Maheshwari,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAESTRO提出了一种针对混合专家（MoE）语言模型的结构化剪枝框架，通过将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，利用其稳态分布捕捉跨层依赖，实现全局感知的重要性评估。在50%压缩率下，平均性能保持率超越现有方法10.61%，且跨任务方差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型虽推理高效，但全部专家参数常驻内存造成部署瓶颈；传统结构化剪枝方法针对稠密Transformer设计，使用基于局部启发式的专家重要性评估，忽视了MoE路由的相互依赖特性。

Method: 将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，利用其平稳分布编码跨层依赖关系，生成全局感知的专家重要性启发式指标，从而指导结构化剪枝。

Result: 在涵盖安全性、偏见与伦理等五个不同领域的评估中，MAESTRO在50%压缩率下平均性能保持率领先基线方法10.61%，且跨任务方差显著降低。

Conclusion: 全局感知、与路由机制一致的结构化剪枝能够产生跨异构任务更稳定泛化的模型。

Abstract: Sparsely-activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models achieve remarkable inference efficiency by activating only a small fraction of parameters per token, yet their full expert banks reside in memory at all times, creating a prohibitive deployment bottleneck. Existing structured pruning methods, largely designed for dense transformers, assess expert importance using locally derived heuristics that are blind to the interdependent nature of MoE routing. We introduce MAESTRO (Markov-chain Approximated Expert Sparsification via Transition-based ROuting), a structured pruning framework designed for MoE architectures that models autoregressive expert activation trajectories as Ergodic Markov chains whose stationary distributions encode cross-layer dependencies, yielding a globally aware importance heuristic. Evaluated across five diverse domains including Safety, Bias, and Ethics, MAESTRO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 10.61% in average performance retention under a strict 50% compression regime, while exhibiting substantially lower cross-task variance, indicating that global, routing-congruent pruning produces models that generalize more consistently across heterogeneous tasks.

</details>


### [39] [DominoTree: Conditional Tree-Structured Drafting with Domino for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08642)
*Saw S. Lin,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DominoTree是一种无需训练的投机解码方法，通过结合Domino的条件非因子化校正和最佳优先树搜索，在每个温度下均实现了最高的平均接受长度（最高10.7 tokens/轮），并在Qwen3-4B上达到6.6倍加速，相比Domino解码器吞吐量提升9-10%，相比DDTree/CaDDTree在T=0时提升24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法存在局限：块扩散草稿器（如DFlash）仅建模逐位置边缘分布，无法捕捉路径依赖；最佳优先树方法（如DDTree）使用因子化公式，无法表示非因子化的条件校正。Domino虽引入GRU条件校正但未利用树结构。作者旨在结合两者优势，提出一种无需训练、能高效利用条件校正信息构建草稿树的方法。

Method: 提出DominoTree方法：1）基于Domino的条件非因子化校正（沿根到节点路径计算）对候选草稿进行评分；2）通过限制每个节点仅保留Top-M候选使计算可行；3）使用GPU原生CUDA图构建器实现草稿树构建，与Python参考实现比特一致，保证接受率不变且构建开销低。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上8个基准测试中：最高6.6倍加速；所有温度下平均接受长度最高（10.7 tokens/轮）；相比Domino解码器吞吐量提升9-10%（Alpaca上+22%）；在各温度下均优于DDTree/CaDDTree。在Qwen3-8B上：所有温度下保持最高接受长度；T=0时吞吐量比DDTree高24%；更高温度时优势收窄至持平或略低，但仍优于DFlash和Domino。

Conclusion: DominoTree通过将非因子化条件校正融入最佳优先树搜索，在无需额外训练的情况下，在多个模型和温度设置下取得了最高的接受长度和吞吐量，证明了结合条件路径依赖与树结构搜索的有效性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting several tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash produce
  a draft block in one pass but model only per-position marginals; best-first tree methods such as DDTree expand candidate trees from those marginals. The
  released Domino drafter adds a GRU-based causal correction that makes each draft token's distribution path-dependent, a structure DDTree's factorized
  formulation cannot represent. We introduce DominoTree, a training-free best-first draft tree scored by Domino's conditional, non-factorized correction
  along each root-to-node path, made practical by restricting the per-node correction to a candidate top-M. On Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, DominoTree
  reaches up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length of any evaluated method, up to 10.7 tokens per round, at every
  temperature we test. DominoTree constructs its tree with a GPU-native, CUDA-graph builder that is bit-identical to a reference Python implementation, so
  acceptance is unchanged, while keeping per-round tree construction cheap. With this builder as default, DominoTree wins throughput over the released
  Domino decoder at every temperature, 9-10% overall on Qwen3-4B and up to +22% on Alpaca, and over DDTree/CaDDTree at every temperature we test. On Qwen3-
  8B, DominoTree keeps the highest accepted length at every temperature and adds a decisive throughput win at T=0, +24% over DDTree; at higher temperature
  that edge over DDTree/CaDDTree narrows to a tie and a small loss, while its Overall aggregate wins over DFlash and Domino persist.

</details>


### [40] [UltraX: Refining Pre-Training Data at Scale with Adaptive Programmatic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08646)
*Xinlong Zhao,Dongsheng Liu,Hengyu Zhao,Zixuan Fu,Zheng Wang,Jie Cai,Jie Zhou,Qiang Ma,Xuanhe Zhou,Xu Han,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UltraX提出了一种基于函数调用的预训练数据精炼框架，通过引入插入操作补全编辑函数空间，结合自适应提示优化、行对齐映射和动态上下文替换等技术，实现了大规模预训练数据的细粒度实例级编辑，在数据质量和效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着训练数据接近物理极限，Scaling Laws的收益逐渐减弱，提升LLMs越来越依赖更高质量的数据利用。然而现有的大规模语料精炼方法存在局限性：基于规则的方法受限于固定启发式规则，难以处理实例级差异；基于LLM的方法虽然质量较高，但无法满足大规模数据处理的高效性和可靠性要求。

Method: UltraX框架包含三部分：1）可靠的程序监督生成管道，包括数据集自适应提示优化、行对齐映射（Line Alignment Mapping）和动态上下文替换（Dynamic Context Replacement）；2）通过低置信度样本过滤和基于操作组合的比例控制采样来提升监督质量和稳定训练分布；3）推理执行阶段通过滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合和系统后处理来规范输出，提升大规模执行的稳定性和可靠性。

Result: UltraX在所有语料库上实现了最高的平均性能，并且用更少的训练token就能匹配或超越基线方法，展现出更强的数据效率和精炼可靠性。

Conclusion: UltraX通过引入插入操作补全了编辑函数空间，并设计了可靠的程序监督生成与执行 pipeline，解决了大规模预训练数据精炼中质量、效率和可靠性的三重挑战，为后Scaling Law时代的数据利用提供了新思路。

Abstract: As available training data approaches its physical limit, gains from Scaling Laws have begun to diminish. Consequently, improving Large Language Models (LLMs) now depends less on data expansion and more on higher-quality data utilization. However, in the context of large-scale corpora, existing refinement methodologies face significant limitations in quality, efficiency, and reliability: Rule-based approaches are constrained by fixed heuristics and struggle with instance-level variations; LLM-based approaches improve quality but fail to meet the efficiency and reliability requirements of large-scale data processing. To address these challenges, we propose UltraX, a function-calling refinement framework for large-scale pre-training data that completes the editing function space by introducing insertion in addition to deletion and modification, enabling fine-grained instance-level editing. Specifically, UltraX builds a reliable program-supervision generation pipeline. In this pipeline, dataset-adaptive prompt optimization first guides an expert LLM to produce high-quality end-to-end refined texts, and Line Alignment Mapping and Dynamic Context Replacement then convert original-refined text pairs into structured program supervision. Meanwhile, UltraX improves supervision quality and stabilizes the training distribution with low-confidence example filtering and ratio-controlled sampling by operation combination. During inference and execution, it normalizes and validates model outputs through sliding-window prediction, global operation aggregation, and systematic post-processing, improving the stability and reliability of large-scale execution. Experiments show that UltraX achieves the highest average performance across all corpora and also matches or surpasses baselines with fewer training tokens, demonstrating stronger data efficiency and refinement reliability.

</details>

*Xiaoshuai Song,Liancheng Zhang,Kangzhi Zhao,Yutao Zhu,Zhongyuan Wang,Guanting Dong,Jinghan Yang,Han Li,Kun Gai,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WebSwarm是一个渐进式递归委派框架，通过动态实例化搜索节点、任务分解与递归扩展，在深度和广度搜索任务上超越了单智能体和多智能体基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单智能体ReAct风格代理受限于单一长轨迹和有限上下文，难以同时处理搜索深度和覆盖广度；现有多智能体系统虽通过并行执行和聚合提升了覆盖范围，但在递归深度、协作适应性和基于证据的扩展方面仍存在明显局限。

Method: WebSwarm采用渐进式递归委派框架：1) 动态实例化搜索节点，每个节点耦合局部目标与搜索模式；2) 节点可自行解决问题或递归委派子节点，子节点求解后将证据和结果向上返回；3) 先探测网页上任务相关信息的组织方式以指导节点扩展；4) 在同质兄弟节点间复用过程级经验。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、WideSearch、DeepWideSearch和GISA四个基准测试上，WebSwarm在深度、广度及深度与广度交织的任务中均一致优于单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: WebSwarm通过渐进式递归委派有效解决了深度与广度搜索的权衡问题，消融实验、任务难度分析、网络工具效率评估和模型泛化性分析进一步验证了其有效性，为多智能体搜索系统提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based web search agents are transforming information seeking from simple factoid question answering into complex, deep-and-wide search and research-oriented tasks. A single ReAct-style agent is constrained by one long trajectory and limited context, making it difficult to handle depth and coverage simultaneously. Existing multi-agent systems improve search coverage through parallel execution and aggregation, but still exhibit clear limitations in recursive depth, collaboration adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion. We propose WebSwarm, a progressive recursive delegation framework that jointly constructs task decomposition, recursive expansion, and agent collaboration during inference. WebSwarm dynamically instantiates agentic search nodes, each coupling a local objective with a search mode that specifies how the node should organize search and collaboration. Each node can either solve its objective itself or further delegate child nodes; after solving, it returns evidence and results upward, enabling parent nodes to further expand, revise, or aggregate the search process. To guide this process, WebSwarm first probes how task-relevant information is organized on the web to ground subsequent node expansion, and reuses process-level experience across homogeneous sibling nodes. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus, WideSearch, DeepWideSearch, and GISA show that WebSwarm consistently outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines on deep, wide, and interleaved deep-and-wide tasks. Further analyses of ablation, task difficulty, web tool efficiency, and model generalization explain WebSwarm's effectiveness and provide insights for multi-agent search systems.

</details>


### [42] [Do You Need a Frontier Model as a Citation Verifier? Benchmarking Rubric LLMs for Deep-Research Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08700)
*Ethan Leung,Elias Lumer,Corey Feld,Austin Huber,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度研究系统中引用质量评估的校准问题，通过对8个LLM法官在1248个评估决策上的测试，发现较便宜的模型在源相关性和事实支持两个维度上具有竞争力，但法官之间存在方向性偏差，F1分数无法捕捉这些偏差，而这对强化学习训练至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于强化学习的LLM训练中，LLM法官作为奖励模型评估每个评分标准，但其可信度和偏差尚未被充分研究。本文旨在校准LLM法官在引用质量评估任务中的能力与偏差，以确保其作为奖励信号的有效性。

Method: 在对抗性长文本基准上，对8个来自3个模型系列的现成LLM法官进行了评估，与人工审核的1248个评分决策（其中378个为争议难例）进行对比，从源相关性和事实支持两个维度评估法官性能，并分析了通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率等偏差指标。

Result: GPT-5-mini在源相关性上取得最佳F1得分0.908（κ=0.636）；事实支持维度上所有法官统计上无显著差异；法官之间存在显著的方向性偏差差异（通过率漂移、假阳性率、假阴性率），而F1分数无法捕捉这些偏差。

Conclusion: 校准LLM法官是将引用质量评分作为强化学习奖励信号的前提条件，且这种校准不需要使用最昂贵的模型。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning increasingly relies on an LLM judge to score each rubric criterion, and that judge acts as the reward model during training. Before such a signal can be trusted, we need to know how capable the judge must be and how biased it is. We study this calibration question for citation quality in deep-research systems, where a search-grounded LLM must support each claim it writes with a cited source. Citation quality is a structured rubric task in which each attribution-citation pair is judged along two dimensions that require an LLM, source relevance and factual support. On an adversarial long-form benchmark, we score 8 off-the-shelf LLM judges from 3 model families against gold labels over 1,248 rubric decisions, all of which were human-reviewed and 378 of which were hard cases adjudicated from judge disagreements. Cheaper judges remain competitive across both dimensions, with GPT-5-mini attaining the strongest source-relevance pass-class F1 at 0.908 ($κ$=0.636), while on factual support the judges are statistically indistinguishable (overlapping confidence intervals), so no single model dominates. At comparable F1, the judges still differ substantially in pass-rate drift, false positive rate, and false negative rate. Scalar F1 obscures this directional bias, yet it is exactly what a downstream reinforcement learning loop would reinforce. Calibrating the judge is therefore a prerequisite for using citation rubrics as reward signals, and our results show that this calibration does not require the most expensive available model.

</details>


### [43] [Validity of LLMs as data annotators: AMALIA on authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08731)
*Manuel Pita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过分析葡萄牙国家级语言模型AMALIA-9B在道德权威基础编码任务中的表现，发现其虽然与人类编码者达到较高一致性（F1差距仅6分），但一致性不等于有效性。通过“恢复差距”方法测试发现，当将整体提示分解为编码簿原子条款再重组时，模型性能损失约50%，错误分析表明模型依赖表面相关特征（如对权威人物的道德愤慨）而非理论构念。相比之下，开源多语言大模型在相同条件下表现更好，排除了语料库因素。研究指出国家语言模型需要超越一致性测试，验证其推理路径的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家语言模型（如葡萄牙的AMALIA）在语义一致性测试中表现良好，但对于需要理论推理的构念（如道德基础），一致性不等于有效性。模型可能通过表面特征的相关捷径而非理论规则得出正确编码。作者旨在检验这种“伪一致性”问题，即模型是否真正遵循构念理论还是依赖表面相关线索。

Method: 采用“恢复差距”（recovery gap）方法：将整体提示分解为编码簿的原子条款，再按理论规则重组，比较分解后性能相对于整体提示的损失。如果校准能缩小该差距，说明模型具有一定可迁移性；否则表明构念-模型工具是失败的主要来源。实验在AMALIA-9B上进行，并对比开源多语言大模型在同一葡萄牙语语料库上的表现。

Result: AMALIA-9B在道德权威基础编码任务中，分解恢复仅保留约50%的整体性能；错误分析显示模型依赖表面特征（如权威人物周围的道德愤慨）而非理论构念。相比之下，开源多语言大模型在相同葡萄牙语语料库和指令下能缩小恢复差距，表明问题不在于语料库而在于模型本身。

Conclusion: 国家级语言模型（如AMALIA）可用于大规模筛选和预编码，但尚未能独立可靠地测量需要理论推理的构念。研究呼吁主权LLM基准测试不仅应检验与人类编码者的一致性，还应验证一致性所依据的证据路径（推理过程）的有效性。

Abstract: A national language model offers a linguistic community its own instrument for measuring what its citizens say and value. Portugal's AMALIA, a publicly funded 9B-parameter model for European Portuguese, appears competitive on agreement alone: asked to code the moral foundation of authority, it agrees with trained human coders to within six F1 points of open models eight to thirteen times its size. Yet agreement is reliability, not validity. For theoretical constructs that must be inferred rather than read from surface features, the question is whether the model follows the construct's theory or reaches the right code by correlated shortcuts. We test this with the recovery gap: the loss in performance when a holistic prompt is decomposed into the codebook's atomic clauses and recombined by the theory's explicit rule. If calibration closes that gap, some portability should survive across models and languages; where it does not, the construct-model instrument is the likely locus of failure. We ask whether a calibrated English instrument transfers to AMALIA-9B and to European Portuguese. For one construct and one corpus, it does not. Decomposition recovers only about half of AMALIA's holistic performance, and error analysis suggests reliance on surface correlates, especially moral outrage near authority figures. An open multilingual LLM closes the gap on the same Portuguese corpus under the same instructions, pointing away from the corpus as the main explanation. AMALIA can still screen and pre-code at scale, but it cannot yet measure this construct well enough to stand alone. The study is a single counterexample, not a verdict on national models; it argues that sovereign-LLM benchmark batteries should test not only agreement with human coders, but the evidential route by which that agreement is warranted.

</details>


### [44] [UniClawBench: A Universal Benchmark for Proactive Agents on Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08768)
*Zhekai Chen,Chengqi Duan,Kaiyue Sun,Bohao Li,Yuqing Wang,Manyuan Zhang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniClawBench 是首个面向动态真实环境中主动代理评估的能力驱动型基准，包含 400 项双语真实世界任务，采用基于 Docker 的实时评估和闭环多轮反馈策略，系统性地解耦了基础模型能力与框架设计对代理性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖于沙盒环境和单轮评估范式，其基于场景的任务分类在同一类别中混合了多种模型能力，难以定位代理失败的根因。

Method: 构建基于五种基础能力（技能使用、探索、长上下文推理、多模态理解、跨平台协调）的基准，设计 400 项双语真实任务；在实时 Docker 容器中使用细粒度逐步完成检查点进行评估；设计闭环评估策略，包含执行代理、隐藏监督代理和用户代理，模拟真实多轮人类反馈并避免泄露评分标准；在多种代理框架下评估 SOTA 模型以解耦基础模型能力与框架设计。

Result: 通过跨模型和跨框架的全面比较，展示了基础模型能力和代理框架设计如何共同影响真实环境中的性能表现。

Conclusion: UniClawBench 有效解决了现有基准的局限性，为主动代理在真实动态环境中的评估提供了更清晰的能力导向分析框架。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models and multimodal large language models has accelerated the emergence of proactive agents capable of operating everyday tools and assisting users in real-world environments. However, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate such agents effectively, as they often rely on sandboxed environments and single-turn evaluation paradigms. Moreover, their scenario-based task taxonomies mix multiple model capabilities within the same task category, making it difficult to identify the root causes of agent failures. To address these limitations, we introduce UniClawBench, the first capability-driven benchmark designed to evaluate proactive agents in dynamic, real-world settings. UniClawBench is built around five foundational model capabilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Based on these capabilities, we design 400 bilingual real-world tasks. Unlike previous benchmarks that rely on static, pre-recorded answers, our benchmark evaluates agents in live Docker containers using fine-grained, step-by-step completion checkpoints. Furthermore, we design a closed-loop evaluation strategy comprising an executor agent, a hidden supervisor agent, and a user agent to simulate realistic multi-turn human feedback without leaking grading criteria. To disentangle base model capabilities from framework-level design choices, we evaluate state-of-the-art models under multiple agent frameworks. Through comprehensive comparisons across both models and frameworks, we show how base model capabilities and agent framework designs jointly shape performance in real-world environments. To facilitate future research, we make our benchmark and code publicly available at https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench.

</details>

### [45] [Controllability-Aware Adversarial Examples Against LLM-Based Network Traffic Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07739)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可控性感知的黑盒迁移攻击框架，用于评估LLM在网络流量分类中的对抗鲁棒性。通过将网络流特征分为直接可控、间接可控和不可控三类，限制扰动仅作用于直接可控特征，使用XGBoost替代模型生成多种对抗样本并迁移至7个LLM和2个传统ML目标。实验发现LLM的迁移脆弱性显著但具有数据集和比较器依赖性，并观察到一致的跨架构迁移层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被探索用于网络入侵检测分类器，但其在现实攻击者约束下的对抗鲁棒性尚不清楚，现有研究缺乏对网络通信语义约束的考虑。

Method: 提出可控性感知的黑盒迁移攻击框架：将流特征按网络通信语义分为DC、IC、UC三类，仅扰动DC特征；使用共享XGBoost替代模型生成有限差分PGD、贪心坐标扰动和NES三种对抗样本；迁移攻击至7个LLM和2个传统ML目标，在5个IDS基准数据集上评估。

Result: 在27个LLM配置和50万+对抗样本上，LLM迁移脆弱性显著但具数据集/比较器依赖性；梯度与基于分数的扰动迁移效果优于贪心扰动；跨替代模型验证结果一致；约束违反率为0%。

Conclusion: LLM在流量分类任务中存在显著的对抗迁移脆弱性，但脆弱程度高度依赖具体数据集和比较基准；所提框架通过语义约束确保了攻击的现实可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored as network intrusion detection classifiers, but their adversarial robustness under realistic attacker constraints remains unclear. We present a controllability-aware black-box transfer framework for LLM-based network traffic classifiers. The framework partitions flow features into directly controllable (DC), indirectly controllable (IC), and uncontrollable (UC) groups according to network communication semantics, then restricts perturbations to DC features while freezing IC/UC features. Using a shared XGBoost surrogate, we generate finite-difference PGD, greedy coordinate-wise, and NES adversarial examples and transfer them to seven LLM targets and two conventional ML targets across five IDS benchmarks from 1999 to 2022. Across 27 valid LLM configurations and over 500,000 adversarial examples, we find that LLM transfer vulnerability is substantial but dataset- and comparator-dependent. Compared with LightGBM, LLMs are more vulnerable on RT-IoT2022 and CIC-IDS-2018, comparable on NSL-KDD and UNSW-NB15, and less vulnerable on HIKARI-2021; compared with the averaged ML baseline, LLMs show higher ASR on all five datasets. We further observe a consistent cross-architecture transfer hierarchy: gradient- and score-based perturbations transfer more effectively than greedy perturbations across all 27 LLM cells and 9/10 ML cells. Cross-surrogate validation with tree, neural, and linear surrogates yields similar LLM ASR, reducing evidence that the findings are XGBoost-specific. Constraint violation rate is 0\% by construction.

</details>


### [46] [Forensic Schema for Psychological Manipulation in Cyber Fraud: LLM-Driven Victim Reports Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07751)
*Zikai Alex Wen,Corrazon Ogot,Juan Li,Yan Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种包含35个问题、4大类别、11种操纵指标及加密货币证据字段的网络犯罪取证分类模式，应用于10,994份受害者报告。LLM注释与人工注释一致性（κ=0.69）匹配了人工间一致性（κ=0.68），揭示了不同欺诈类型具有显著不同的操纵特征（Cramer's V最高0.790）。但证据审计发现操作技术检测可靠，受害者叙述在可操作细节上差异大，区块链标识符几乎缺失，指向AI辅助受害者问询作为解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络犯罪分类模式仅捕获接触元数据和金融交易，忽略了犯罪者使用的心理操纵技术，导致取证分析不完整。

Method: 提出4类别35问题的取证分类模式，增加11种操纵指标和加密货币证据字段；基于LLM对10,994份受害者报告进行注释；与两名人类注释者验证一致性；进行基于理由的证据审计。

Result: LLM-人工一致性（κ=0.69）匹配人工间一致性（κ=0.68）；各主要欺诈类型具有统计显著的独特操纵特征（Cramer's V最高0.790）；操纵技术检测可靠，但受害者叙述在可操作细节上差异大，区块链标识符几乎缺失。

Conclusion: AI辅助受害者问询结合分类模式的跟进问题是最直接的解决方案；分层注释策略可作为LLM从其他取证文本领域提取信息的可复用模板。

Abstract: Existing cybercrime classification schemas capture contact metadata and financial transactions but omit the psychological manipulation techniques perpetrators employ. We present a forensic schema (four categories, 35 questions) adding 11 manipulation indicators and cryptocurrency evidence fields to established forensic foundations. Applied to 10,994 victim reports via large language model (LLM)-driven annotation and validated against two human annotators (mean LLM-human $κ= 0.69$, matching inter-annotator $κ= 0.68$), the schema revealed a statistically distinct manipulation profile for each major fraud type (Cramer's $V$ up to $0.790$). A rationale-based evidence audit nonetheless exposed a forensic detail gap: detection of manipulation techniques was reliable, but victim narratives varied widely in the actionable detail supporting each Yes answer, and blockchain-specific identifiers were nearly absent. These findings point to AI-assisted victim intake with schema-informed follow-up questions as the most direct way to close the gap. The tiered annotation strategy also provides a reusable template for LLM-based extraction from other forensic text domains.

</details>


### [47] [ScopeJudge: Cost-Aware Pre-Execution Gating for Offensive Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07774)
*Shane Caldwell,Max Harley,Ads Dawson,Michael Kouremetis,Vincent Abruzzo,Will Pearce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ScopeJudge基准，用于评估LLM安全代理在执行前通过轻量级法官模型检测工具调用是否超出用户定义的范围（scope），发现静态策略无法胜任，必须基于用户请求进行条件监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在 offensive security 任务中，一次超出范围的工具调用可能破坏客户边界、影响生产或导致漏洞赏金失效。范围边界由用户请求声明且需从意图推断，但固定安全策略无法预先枚举所有合法/非法目标，亟需一种动态、可扩展的范围 enforcement 方法。

Method: 提出 pre-execution gating：一个廉价、可信的LLM法官在每次工具调用执行前进行审查。构建ScopeJudge基准（4,897次调用，7.7%违规），由专业渗透测试员标注。评估8种法官模型在5种转录策略下的成本-准确率Pareto前沿。

Result: 静态策略在不知用户请求时召回率接近零；推荐两个操作点：成本敏感配置和高召回优先配置。法官间Fleiss kappa=0.64，专家参考F1=0.78。

Conclusion: 范围（scope）存在于用户请求中，必须进行请求条件监控；静态策略结构上不足以应对范围 enforcement。ScopeJudge数据集支持实时监控和可扩展监督。

Abstract: As LLM agents take on offensive security work, a single out-of-scope tool call can breach a client's engagement boundary, disrupt production, or void a bug-bounty finding. Unlike a fixed safety policy, the boundary that matters is declared in the user's request and must be inferred from intent. That challenge is sharpened by the adversarial nature of offensive security: the same tool call is in or out of scope depending not on the action itself but on the target it touches and the context in which it runs, which no fixed policy can enumerate in advance. We study pre-execution gating: a cheap, trusted LLM judge inspects each call proposed by a strong, swappable agent, and accepts or rejects it before it runs. We introduce ScopeJudge, a benchmark of 4,897 tool calls (7.7% scope violations) from agent trajectories on tasks engineered to tempt agents out of scope and labeled at the call level by professional penetration testers, with substantial inter-grader agreement (Fleiss kappa = 0.64) that sets an expert agreement reference point of F1 = 0.78. We evaluate eight judge models under five transcript strategies, varying how much context the judge sees, from the static policy alone to the full raw transcript, and chart the resulting cost-accuracy Pareto frontier. We find that a static policy is structurally insufficient for scope enforcement: blind to the user's request, judge recall collapses to near zero, confirming that scope lives in the request and that request-conditioned monitoring is necessary. Because a missed violation costs more than a spurious rejection, we report precision, recall, and F1 separately and recommend two operating points: a cost-sensitive configuration and a recall-first one for high-stakes deployments. We release the ScopeJudge dataset to support real-time monitoring and scalable oversight of autonomous security agents.

</details>


### [48] [Open Models, Open Risks: Measuring Unsafe Generation in Text-to-Image Models In the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07827)
*Peilin Han,Yang Liu,Yilong Yang,Jingchun Zhang,Teng Li,Jianfeng Ma,Zhuo Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，揭示了现实世界中文本到图像（T2I）模型越狱攻击的安全现状。研究发现，传统的基于检测器的越狱指标会高估实际风险，因此提出了改进的ASR指标（Advanced ASR）以更准确地评估语义有效且视觉合理的违规生成。通过对Hugging Face上200多个T2I模型的评估，发现大多数下游模型即使没有显式的后处理安全防护，仍保留了一定程度的安全性，但同时也识别出一批高风险模型，包括明确NSFW导向的发布以及表面无害但存在不安全行为的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2I越狱安全研究主要在受控实验室环境下进行，使用的模型数量有限，导致对快速增长的现实世界T2I生态系统的安全现状缺乏了解。此外，现有检测器指标不适用于现实评估，且现实风险不仅来自对抗性提示，还来自不安全的发布实践和不安全的模型衍生。

Method: 首先指出现有检测器越狱指标因语义漂移和生成伪影高估风险，提出Advanced ASR指标以捕捉语义有效且视觉合理的违规生成。然后使用该指标对Hugging Face上200多个T2I模型在三种代表性越狱攻击下进行评估。

Result: 许多下游模型即使没有显式后处理安全防护仍保留一定安全性，安全退化既非普遍也非均匀。同时识别出高风险模型，包括明确NSFW导向的发布和表面良性但通过系统评估才暴露不安全行为的模型。

Conclusion: 揭示了现实世界T2I模型安全状态的复杂图景，将高风险案例报告给Hugging Face，为后续安全治理提供依据。

Abstract: Existing safety studies on text-to-image (T2I) jailbreaks are largely conducted in controlled in-the-lab settings, typically on a small number of canonical models. As a result, the current safety status of the rapidly growing in-the-wild T2I ecosystem remains unclear. This uncertainty is amplified by two factors: existing detector-based metrics are designed for controlled evaluation, and in-the-wild risks may arise not only from adversarial prompting, but also from unsafe release practices and unsafe model derivatives.
  In this paper, we present a large-scale empirical study of in-the-wild T2I safety through the lens of jailbreak. We first show that detector-only jailbreak metrics substantially overestimate practical risk over in the wild due to semantic drift and generation artifacts, and we introduce Advanced ASR to better capture semantically valid and visually plausible unsafe generation. Using this refined metric, we evaluate 200+ in-the-wild T2I models from Hugging Face under three representative jailbreak attacks. Our results show that many downstream models retain a non-trivial degree of safety even without explicit post-hoc safeguards, indicating that safety degradation in the wild is neither universal nor uniform. At the same time, we identify a set of high-risk models, including explicitly NSFW-oriented releases as well as seemingly benign models whose unsafe behavior is only exposed through systematic evaluation. We further trace these models to their release context and report high-risk cases to Hugging Face.

</details>


### [49] [Closed-Loop Dynamic Validator Node Scaling in Private Substrate Blockchains Using Takagi-Sugeno Fuzzy Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07901)
*Thandile Nododile,Ayinde M. Usman,Clement N. Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Takagi-Sugeno模糊推理系统的控制器，用于动态调整私有区块链网络中的验证节点数量，通过读取区块生成时间、区块大小和活跃节点数等实时参数，输出效率评分和扩展建议（增加、维持或减少节点），并在10节点Substrate区块链网络上验证了其相较于阈值基线方法的更稳定扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有区块链网络采用固定节点配置，无法适应动态变化的工作负载：节点过多造成资源浪费，节点过少则降低区块生成和确认速度，且最优验证节点数量难以确定。

Method: 构建Takagi-Sugeno模糊推理系统，使用三角形隶属函数和三个语言变量（区块生成时间、区块大小、活跃节点数），基于完整的27条规则库和乘积t-范数聚合。关键贡献在于基于实验平台实测数据对隶属函数进行经验性重新校准，而非使用理论极值。

Result: 在4、7、10个活跃节点的配置下，控制器产生可区分的操作轮廓；闭环实验中控制器能双向自动调整验证节点参与数量；与三种阈值基线方法相比，扩展振荡更少且区块生成时间相当。

Conclusion: TS模糊推理能够支持私有区块链部署中的自主验证节点管理，在扩展稳定性方面优于阈值方法。

Abstract: Private blockchain networks run with fixed node configurations that cannot adapt to changing workload conditions. Too many nodes serving a light workload waste resources; too few nodes facing heavy demand slow block production and degrade finalisation. The right validator count is hard to determine, as it depends on overlapping factors that shift over time. This paper presents a Takagi-Sugeno (TS) fuzzy inference system that reads live blockchain parameters (block production time, block size, and active node count) and outputs a continuous efficiency score alongside a scaling recommendation: Scale Up, Maintain, or Scale Down. The controller uses triangular membership functions across three linguistic variables, evaluated through a complete 27-rule base with product t-norm aggregation. A key contribution is an empirical recalibration of the membership functions, anchoring linguistic terms to the observed operating range of the testbed rather than to theoretical extremes. The system is evaluated on a 10-node Substrate blockchain network storing real smart water meter data hashes from the Queensland Government open data portal. Statistical analysis across configurations of 4, 7, and 10 active nodes confirms that the controller produces distinct operational profiles reflecting each configuration's provisioning state. In closed-loop experiments, the controller autonomously adjusts validator participation in both directions, activating validators under rising load and removing them under over-provisioning, converging to the same stable equilibrium from both directions. Compared against three threshold-based baselines, it shows fewer scaling oscillations while maintaining comparable block production times. Results show that TS fuzzy inference can support autonomous validator management in private blockchain deployments, with stable scaling behaviour threshold approaches cannot match.

</details>


### [50] [Mechanistic Interpretability of LLM Jailbreaks via Internal Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07903)
*Anupam Wagle,Ifrat Ikhtear Uddin,Chaowei Zhang,Longwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于内部计算图的机制性框架，通过构建和对比干净与攻击提示下的计算图，系统揭示对抗攻击如何改变LLM内部推理（抑制安全组件、产生攻击特征、重路由计算路径），并实现从描述性归因到因果诊断的转变，进而通过因果干预提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过输入-输出行为或归因方法分析LLM对抗攻击失败案例，对对抗扰动如何改变模型内部推理机制的理解极为有限，导致不安全或错误行为背后的机制仍不清晰。

Method: 提出基于配对内部计算图的机制性诊断框架：(i) 将计算分解为不变、抑制和涌现结构；(ii) 识别与失败模式相关的反复出现的脆弱性主题；(iii) 对节点、路径和子图进行因果干预，直接评估其对攻击成功的贡献。

Result: 在多个开源LLM和多种对抗/越狱基准上的实验表明，内部计算图的结构偏差与不安全行为高度相关；针对识别的脆弱性主题进行因果干预可显著提升模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 内部计算图为理解、诊断和缓解LLM脆弱性提供了原则性基础，实现了从描述性归因到因果诊断的范式转变。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable capabilities but remain highly vulnerable to adversarial prompts and jailbreak attacks. Existing approaches primarily analyze these failures through input-output behaviors or attribution methods, offering limited insight into how adversarial perturbations alter the model's internal reasoning. Consequently, the mechanisms underlying unsafe or incorrect behaviors remain poorly understood. We introduce a mechanistic framework for diagnosing LLM vulnerabilities using paired internal computation graphs, which represent prompt-specific inference as structured causal interactions among latent features. By constructing and aligning computation graphs for clean and attacked prompts, we reveal that adversarial attacks induce systematic transformations of internal reasoning, including suppression of safety-relevant components, emergence of attack-specific features, and rerouting of computation paths. Building on this representation, we propose a unified framework that (i) decomposes computation into invariant, suppressed, and emergent structures, (ii) identifies recurring vulnerability motifs associated with failure modes, and (iii) performs causal interventions on nodes, paths, and subgraphs to directly evaluate their contributions to attack success. This enables a transition from descriptive attribution to causal diagnosis of model failures. Experiments across multiple open-source LLMs and diverse adversarial and jailbreak benchmarks demonstrate that structural deviations in internal computation graphs strongly correlate with unsafe behaviors. Furthermore, targeted interventions on identified vulnerability motifs improve model robustness, establishing internal computation graphs as a principled foundation for understanding, diagnosing, and mitigating LLM vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [51] [KS-CFA: Control-Flow Attestation via Symbolic Replay Against Control-Flow Bending Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07926)
*Zhanyu Sha,Konstantinos Markantonakis,Carlton Shepherd,Amir Rafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: KS-CFA是一种新型控制流证明方案，通过符号执行和识别由输入驱动的控制流相关变量，检测四种跳转类型上的控制流弯曲攻击，无需枚举海量测量空间或依赖底层硬件修改，在RISC-V Keystone TEE上实现，验证开销为6.7-32.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制流证明(CFA)方案难以检测控制流弯曲(CFB)攻击，且存在需枚举海量测量空间、仅覆盖部分攻击类型或依赖低级硬件修改等重大缺陷。

Method: 结合符号执行与选择性识别由输入源驱动的控制流相关变量（即从外部输入直接读取的控制流相关状态子集），证明方在TEE内记录控制流轨迹和外部输入，验证方通过单路径符号执行重放报告路径，预测每次跳转并定位偏差。

Result: 在RISC-V Keystone TEE及Embench-IoT基准测试上实现，证明方开销在QEMU上为6.8-20.5倍，在FPGA上为6.7-32.2倍，验证无需路径或值的枚举。

Conclusion: KS-CFA有效检测四种跳转类型的CFB攻击，避免了现有方案的主要局限，在开销与完备性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Control-flow attestation (CFA) enables a remote entity to verify program execution on a target device by monitoring control-flow behaviour at runtime. However, control-flow bending (CFB) attacks remain difficult to detect, where an adversary steers execution along legal edges of the program's control-flow graph by corrupting branch flags, loop counters, and other runtime data. Existing solutions impose significant drawbacks: they require enumerating vast measurement spaces, cover only a reduced subset of attacks, or rely on low-level hardware modifications. In this work, we present KS-CFA, a new CFA scheme that detects CFB attacks across four transfer types (indirect calls, conditional and indirect jumps, and returns) without those costs. To this end, we combine symbolic execution and selective identification of input-sourced control-flow dependent variables: a strict subset of control-flow-relevant state whose values are directly read from external input. The proving device records, inside a trusted execution environment (TEE), a control-flow trace and the external inputs that determine relevant run-time variables. The verifier then replays the reported path through single-path symbolic execution, predicting each transfer and localising divergences that signal an attack. We implement and evaluate KS-CFA using the RISC-V Keystone TEE and Embench-IoT on QEMU and a Rocket-based FPGA platform (NiteFury II). Prover-side overhead relative to unattested execution ranges from 6.8-20.5x on QEMU and 6.7-32.2x on the FPGA, and verification requires no path or value enumeration.

</details>


### [52] [Who Broke the System? Failure Localization in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07989)
*Yufei Xia,Anjun Gao,Yueyang Quan,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentLocate是一个针对基于LLM的多智能体系统的故障定位框架，能够将故障归因到特定智能体和最早的关键决策步骤，结合LLM评判机制、多视角验证和置信度感知聚合，并通过轻量级微调优化评判效果，在两个基准测试上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在执行复杂任务时，由于长程交互和智能体行为紧密耦合，当系统执行失败时，难以定位是哪个智能体在哪个步骤出现了不可逆的错误方向。

Method: AgentLocate包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于LLM的评判机制；(2) 多视角验证（由独立评估者从不同角度评估）；(3) 置信度感知的聚合策略整合多方反馈；并通过轻量级微调自适应优化评判器，提升归因质量。

Result: 在两个互补基准测试（覆盖多样化任务、智能体配置和轨迹长度）上，AgentLocate在识别责任智能体和故障步骤方面均优于现有故障定位方法，同时保持较低的token消耗和运行时间。

Conclusion: AgentLocate有效解决了多智能体系统中的细粒度故障定位难题，通过多视角验证与置信度聚合相结合的策略，实现了高精度归因和良好的效率平衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-agent systems enable complex problem solving through coordinated reasoning and action, but their distributed structure also introduces new challenges in diagnosing system-level failures. When an execution fails, identifying which agent is responsible and at what point the trajectory first becomes irreversibly misdirected is difficult due to long-horizon interactions and tightly coupled agent behaviors. In this paper, we study the problem of failure localization in LLM-based multi-agent systems and present AgentLocate, a framework that attributes failures to both a specific agent and the earliest decisive step. AgentLocate combines an LLM-based judging mechanism with multi-perspective verification by independent evaluators, whose assessments are aggregated using a confidence-aware strategy. The resulting feedback is further used to adapt the judge through lightweight fine-tuning, improving attribution quality. We evaluate AgentLocate on two complementary benchmarks covering diverse tasks, agent configurations, and trajectory lengths. Experimental results show that AgentLocate consistently outperforms existing failure localization methods in identifying both responsible agents and failure steps, while remaining efficient in terms of token usage and running time.

</details>


### [53] [Beware What You Autocomplete: Forensic Attribution of Backdoored Code Completions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08011)
*Anjun Gao,Yueyang Quan,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CodeTracer是一个后门攻击取证框架，能从恶意代码补全中追溯至引发该行为的微调数据，在三种漏洞案例和十种后门攻击上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型驱动的代码补全系统易受后门攻击影响，现有防御技术难以检测和缓解自适应且复杂的后门攻击，亟需一种能够追溯恶意行为来源的取证方法。

Method: CodeTracer在仅依赖微调语料库和报告的错误补全事件的条件下，首先从受损输出中提取结构化行为指纹，然后将搜索范围缩小到语义相关的代码样本，最后利用基于LLM的推理将不安全逻辑归因到特定的后门数据。

Result: 在三种代表性漏洞案例、十种后门攻击和十六个竞争基线上的评估表明，CodeTracer持续实现了高取证准确率、低误识别率以及对自适应攻击的强大鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CodeTracer为代码补全模型的后门攻击提供了一种有效的取证框架，能够在实际部署约束下可靠地追溯恶意行为至后门微调数据。

Abstract: Large language models have enabled powerful code completion systems that assist developers by predicting subsequent lines of code. However, these models remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where malicious fine-tuning data covertly implants unsafe behaviors. Despite advances in defensive techniques, adaptive and sophisticated backdoor attacks still evade detection and mitigation. We present CodeTracer, a forensic framework that traces malicious code completions back to the backdoor fine-tuning data responsible for them. Operating under realistic post-deployment constraints, CodeTracer relies solely on the fine-tuning corpus and the reported miscompletion event. It extracts a structured behavioral fingerprint from the compromised output, narrows the search to semantically relevant code samples, and employs LLM-based reasoning to attribute unsafe logic to specific backdoor data. Extensive evaluations across three representative vulnerability cases and ten backdoor attacks, along with sixteen competitive baselines, demonstrate that CodeTracer consistently achieves high forensic accuracy, low false identification rates, and strong robustness against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [54] [Degree-Constrained Interval Optimization for Minimax Polynomial Approximation in Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08042)
*Jiheon Woo,Donggyun Ryu,Yongjune Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布感知的区间优化方法，结合极小极大多项式与域扩展函数/多项式，在均方误差目标下平衡同态加密中多项式近似的区间宽度与逼近误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同态加密的隐私保护推理中，非多项式激活函数需用多项式近似。极小极大多项式的近似区间是关键超参数：宽区间提升对大幅值输入的鲁棒性，但增加最小最大逼近误差。现有方法缺乏对区间选择的系统优化，本文旨在形式化这一权衡。

Method: 1) 将区间选择建模为分布感知的均方误差优化问题；2) 引入域扩展函数及其可同态实现的多项式版本来抑制区间外的多项式外推；3) 推导基于域扩展函数的可解析处理代理目标，捕获区间内逼近误差与区间外裁剪误差的权衡；4) 通过实现误差分解及上界，将代理目标与可同态实现的域扩展多项式建立联系。

Result: 建立了域扩展函数代理目标与域扩展多项式实现之间的误差上界，为分布感知的区间优化提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 该方法通过分布感知的区间优化结合域扩展多项式，有效平衡了同态加密中多项式近似的精度与鲁棒性，为隐私保护推理中的激活函数近似提供了更系统的设计框架。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables privacy-preserving inference under arithmetic constraints that restrict encrypted evaluation to additions and multiplications. As a result, non-polynomial activation functions must be replaced by polynomial approximations. Among polynomial approximation methods, minimax approximation, typically computed by the Remez algorithm, is a standard approach because it minimizes the maximum approximation error over a given design interval. For minimax polynomial design, the approximation interval is a critical hyperparameter: a wider interval improves robustness to large-magnitude inputs while increasing the minimax approximation error under a fixed degree budget. In this paper, we formulate this trade-off as a distribution-aware interval optimization problem, where the approximation interval is chosen to minimize the mean-squared error (MSE) with respect to the pre-activation distribution of interest. To effectively control outside-interval inputs, we combine minimax polynomials with domain extension functions (DEFs) and their HE-realizable polynomial counterparts, domain extension polynomials (DEPs), which approximate a clipping operation outside the design interval and thereby suppress uncontrolled polynomial extrapolation. We first derive an analytically tractable DEF-based proxy objective that captures the trade-off between within-interval minimax approximation error and outside-interval clipping error. We then connect this idealized objective to HE-realizable DEP constructions through an implementation-error decomposition with an accompanying upper bound.

</details>


### [55] [zkComposer: Decomposing Proof Construction to Scale zkML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08095)
*Pawan Kumar Sanjaya,Christina Giannoula,Valdy Oktavian,Mehdi Saeedi,Gabor Sines,Gururaj Saileshwar,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: zkComposer提出模块化证明框架，将零知识机器学习推理证明分解为独立子证明，通过并行化子证明生成，相比现有系统zkCNN和zkGPT将证明时间最多减少6.84倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有zkML系统证明生成成本高昂，且因依赖单一整体证明（monolithic proof）导致并行化受限——增加线程数无法显著降低证明时间。

Method: zkComposer将zkML推理的正确性证明分解为多个独立子证明，每个子证明覆盖模型的部分计算（如连续层子集）；相邻子证明通过边界层激活值的共享承诺进行密码学链接，保证与整体证明相同的安全性，无需额外链接证明或修改底层密码学原语。

Result: 在CNN工作负载上，相比zkCNN，证明时间和响应时间最多降低3.25倍；在GPT-2上沿模型层划分时最多降低4.83倍；当同时沿模型层和输入序列划分时，最多降低6.84倍（均相对于zkGPT）。

Conclusion: zkComposer通过解锁额外的并行维度（子证明并行生成），在保持安全性的前提下显著降低了zkML的证明生成时间。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge machine learning (zkML) enables a server to perform verifiable inference while keeping model parameters private from the client. However, existing zkML systems incur prohibitive proof-generation costs. We observe that proof generation exhibits limited parallelism; that is, prover time does not decrease significantly as the number of threads increases. This limitation is because existing systems rely on monolithic proof computation, constructing a single proof for the entire machine learning model. We introduce zkComposer, a modular proof-construction framework that unlocks an additional dimension of parallelism, in addition to the parallelism in existing proof kernels. zkComposer decomposes the zkML proof of correct inference into independent sub-proofs, each covering a subset of the computation for inference e.g., each independent sub-proof can cover a subset of contiguous layers in the ML model. Adjacent sub-proofs are cryptographically linked through shared commitments to the activations from the boundary layer. zkComposer provides the same guarantees as the monolithic proof without requiring additional linking proofs or changes to the underlying cryptographic primitives. We implement zkComposer and evaluate it on three CNNs and GPT-2. We show that, on CNN workloads, zkComposer reduces prover time and response time by up to 3.25x relative to zkCNN [1]. On GPT-2, zkComposer reduces these times by up to 4.83x relative to zkGPT [2], when partitioning along the model layers. When partitioning across both model layers and input sequences in GPT-2, we show that zkComposer reduces prover time and response time by up to 6.84x relative to zkGPT [2].

</details>


### [56] [Securing Autonomous Vehicle Systems via Twin-Aware Federated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08137)
*Zifan Zhang,Minghong Fang,Dianwei Chen,Zhuqing Liu,Prashant Khanduri,Xianfeng Yang,Anupam Das,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecAgg-p²（安全聚合与投毒防御及历史强化框架），通过数字孪生排练学习、历史聚合参数和中心梯度选择机制，抵御联邦强化学习中的投毒攻击，保障自动驾驶场景下的安全聚合与收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦强化学习在动态车辆环境中实现多智能体协作学习与隐私保护，但投毒攻击严重威胁其安全性与可靠性，尤其在安全关键的自动驾驶领域该漏洞尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出SecAgg-p²防御框架，集成数字孪生进行基于排练的学习，利用历史聚合模型参数和选定的中心梯度，确保仅聚合良性数据，从而减轻恶意智能体的影响。

Result: 提供了存在投毒攻击下SecAgg-p²收敛性能的理论保证，并通过模拟真实高速公路环境的数字孪生验证了其在对抗条件下对自动驾驶车辆控制的有效性。

Conclusion: SecAgg-p²能够有效增强面向安全关键驾驶场景的FRL系统的鲁棒性，抵御投毒攻击，保障安全聚合。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning (FRL) is crucial for enabling collaborative learning across multiple agents without sharing raw data, thereby enhancing privacy and scalability in the decision-making process within dynamic vehicular environments. However, poisoning attacks pose a significant threat to the security and reliability of FRL-based systems, particularly in safety-critical autonomous driving, where this vulnerability remains largely unexplored. These attacks can compromise the global control model by subtly injecting malicious system parameters, leading to potential hazards. To counter these challenges, we present \alg (\underline{Sec}ure \underline{A}ggregation with \underline{p}oisoning-\underline{p}revention and historical reinforcement) as a defensive framework aimed at enhancing the robustness of FRL systems designed for safety-critical driving scenarios. \alg strategically integrates digital twins for rehearsal-based learning and leverages historical aggregated model parameters along with a selected central gradient to ensure that only benign data is aggregated, effectively mitigating the influence of malicious agents. Theoretical guarantees are provided for the convergence performance of \alg in the presence of poisoning attacks. We also validate the effectiveness of \alg using developed digital twins that model realistic highway environments to evaluate the control of autonomous vehicles under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [57] [Prismata: Confining Cross-Site Prompt Injection in Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08147)
*Corban Villa,Alp Eren Ozdarendeli,Sijun Tan,Raluca Ada Popa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Prismata 是一种针对自主网页代理的防御机制，通过动态信任推导和结构约束实现上下文最小权限，在无需开发者标注的情况下有效降低提示注入攻击成功率，同时保持良性任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网页代理继承了Web的跨站脚本攻击面，第三方和用户生成内容可通过提示注入劫持代理。核心挑战在于，推导任务特定安全策略需要对页面结构进行推理，而该结构与攻击者内容纠缠在一起。

Method: Prismata 包含两个核心机制：(1) 动态信任推导——受经典完整性模型启发，为页面内容生成权限标签，具有结构约束保证，确保标签只能降权且误标有界；(2) 机械约束——通过遮蔽内容和限制代理能力来强制执行这些标签。

Result: 在已发表的网页代理攻击（包括自适应变体）上，Prismata 大幅降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了良性任务的效用。

Conclusion: Prismata 提出了一种无需开发者标注的上下文最小权限防御方案，有效解决了网页代理面临的结构与内容纠缠导致的提示注入难题。

Abstract: Autonomous web agents promise to automate everyday browsing tasks, but inherit one of the web's oldest attack surfaces. Cross-Site Scripting proved that mixing trusted and untrusted content is dangerous, even on benign pages. Agents resurface this risk by interpreting natural language as instructions, allowing third-party and user-generated content to hijack the agent via prompt injection. The core challenge is that deriving a task-specific security policy requires reasoning over page structure that is entangled with the attacker's content.
  We present Prismata, a defense enforcing contextual least privilege for web agents, constraining both what the agent sees and what it can do. Prismata's dynamic trust derivation produces permission labels for page content, with structural confinement guarantees, inspired by classical integrity models, that bound any labeling errors so that labels can only decrease in privilege and mislabelings are bounded. Prismata's mechanical confinement enforces these labels by redacting content and restricting agent capabilities. Importantly, these mechanisms require no developer annotations, so Prismata supports the long tail of websites. Across recent published web agent attacks, including adaptive variants, Prismata substantially reduces attack success while preserving benign task utility.

</details>


### [58] [Out of Sight: Compression-Aware Content Protection against Agentic Crawlers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08180)
*Xuefei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CAPE 是一个针对基于 LLM 的智能体（Agent）的文本内容保护框架，通过在文本中注入不可见扰动，利用智能体在上下文压缩过程中的信息丢失来保护高价值内容，最高可提升信息损失达 75.8%，且对 LangGraph 和 GitHub Copilot 等实际场景有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的智能体具备推理、总结和记忆能力，给在线内容带来了新的威胁面，传统防御（如访问控制、注入式防御）要么易被绕过，要么降低可读性，亟需一种不破坏人类可读性的内容保护方法。

Method: 将智能体例行的上下文压缩过程作为防御层，注入肉眼不可见的扰动，使得智能体在压缩时产生严重信息丢失。具体通过从可访问的代理压缩器提取破坏性种子扰动，再通过先验引导的进化和偏好校准的候选优先级排序，适配到仅支持查询的目标压缩器，在低查询预算下实现有效保护。

Result: 在三种内容类型和四种压缩设置上，相比最强基线，信息损失提升高达 75.8%，且保护后的内容与原始内容视觉上无法区分。在实际场景（LangGraph 工作流和 GitHub Copilot）中也表现出良好的迁移性。

Conclusion: 揭示了上下文压缩可作为智能体时代内容保护的新防御层，推动了该方向的研究，证明 CAPE 具有通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: The rise of LLM-based agents with reasoning, summarization, and memory capabilities has created a new threat surface for online content that conventional defenses fail to address. Existing defenses like access controls can be circumvented by agents mimicking ordinary browsers, and injection-based defenses often degrade human readability. In this paper, we revisit the agent pipeline and identify context compression, which agents routinely invoke to fit context budgets, as a critical yet overlooked defense layer. We propose CAPE, a framework that protects high-value textual content by injecting invisible perturbations without changing its human-visible surface form, thereby inducing severe information loss during agent compression. CAPE extracts disruptive seed perturbations from an accessible surrogate compressor, then adapts them to query-only target compressors through prior-guided evolution and preference-calibrated candidate prioritization, achieving effective protection under a low query budget. Experiments on three content types and four compression settings show that CAPE improves information loss by up to 75.8% over the strongest baseline while keeping protected content visually indistinguishable from originals. CAPE also transfers to real-world settings, including the LangGraph agent workflow and GitHub Copilot, highlighting its generality and practical value. This paper aims to reveal context compression as a new defense layer, promoting content protection research in the agent era.

</details>


### [59] [MLQENABLER: Enabling Secure Machine Learning Queries over Encrypted Database in Cloud Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08197)
*Xu Zhou,Haoyang Chen,Xinyu Lei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MLQENABLER方案，利用索引辅助方法在加密云数据库上实现安全机器学习查询，在保证安全性的同时仅带来轻微ML性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共云服务商在提供云存储的同时利用客户数据提供ML服务，但云服务商不可信，可能泄露客户敏感数据；直接加密数据虽能保护安全但会使ML无法执行，需要一种既能保证安全又能支持ML查询的方案。

Method: 提出MLQENABLER (ML Queries Enabler) 方案，采用索引辅助方法（index-aid approach），在加密数据库上同时实现安全性和ML查询能力。

Result: 初步实验表明MLQENABLER达到了可接受的安全水平，且仅带来轻微的ML性能下降。

Conclusion: MLQENABLER方案有效解决了加密云数据库上执行ML查询的安全与性能权衡问题，为云存储中安全ML服务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In cloud computing, the public cloud service providers (CSPs) can provide cloud storage as the primary service while providing additional machine learning (ML)-based services by using the clients' data in storage. This business model extends the border of cloud computing services and brings in new business growth possibilities. Although it is promising, the model also brings in security concerns since the public commercial cloud cannot be fully trusted. For example, the public commercial clouds may sell clients' sensitive data to the government or other companies. To address the security concerns, an immediate solution is to require clients to encrypt their datasets before outsourcing to the cloud. However, if a database is formally encrypted, then the database contains only pseudorandom numbers, making it impossible to enable ML over it. In this project, we propose MLQENABLER (ML Queries Enabler) scheme to enable secure ML queries over encrypted database in cloud storage. MLQENABLER employs an index-aid approach to achieve security and ML capability simultaneously. Our initial experiments show that MLQENABLER achieves an acceptable security level while incurring only a slight ML performance degradation.

</details>


### [60] [Threshold Authorization Without Threshold Signatures: Signature-Agnostic MPC Custody](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08226)
*Dariia Porechna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种双门架构，将成员认证与阈值授权分离，使得签名方案可灵活替换（如从ECDSA迁移至后量子方案ML-DSA/SLH-DSA仅需密钥轮换），并支持信息论安全的可编程授权策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码迁移打破了传统阈值签名方案（TSS）的既有模型：哈希签名不支持高效阈值签署，格基阈值协议仍不成熟。现有方案将成员签名与阈值授权耦合，导致协议升级困难。

Method: 双门架构：第一道门用任意EUF-CMA签名方案进行成员认证；第二道门通过Shamir秘密共享生成阈值封印（threshold seal）。封印作为可编程授权计算的基础实例，支持简单法定人数到复杂策略。签名方案作为部署参数，迁移仅需密钥轮换。

Result: 支持后量子迁移无需协议重新设计；成员可使用商品HSM的标准签名API参与；对手即使持有≥t个签名密钥但无数额系数份额也无法伪造封印；低于阈值的保密性达到信息论安全。

Conclusion: 该架构可部署于任何支持可编程验证的资产控制路径（如智能合约、保险库模块、HSM），生成执行层的授权而非原生链签名，为后量子时代的数字资产托管提供了实用的过渡方案。

Abstract: Digital-asset custody has been built on threshold multi-party approval: no operation proceeds unless $t$ of $n$ parties approve, and fewer than t compromised parties can neither authorize nor learn the authorization secret. Threshold signature schemes (TSS) have been the standard mechanism, but the post-quantum transition disrupts this model: standardized hash-based signatures resist efficient threshold signing, and lattice-based threshold protocols remain an emerging research track.
  We present a dual-gate architecture that separates member authentication from threshold authorization.
